    """Get current date."""
    import datetime
    now = datetime.datetime.now()
    WS_CURRENT_DATE_DATA.ws_current_date = int(now.strftime("%Y%m%d"))

def validate_system_C1() -> None:
    """Validate system."""
    if WS_FILE_STATUSES.ws_cust_status != '00':
        print("ERROR: CUSTOMER FILE OPEN FAILED")
        WS_FLAGS.ws_error_flag = 'Y'
    if WS_FILE_STATUSES.ws_acct_status != '00':
        print("ERROR: ACCOUNT FILE OPEN FAILED")
        WS_FLAGS.ws_error_flag = 'Y'

def process_deposits_C1() -> None:
    """Process deposits."""
    print("PROCESSING DEPOSITS...")
    WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ account_master NEXT
        #AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #NOT AT END
        validate_deposit_C1()
        if WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
            post_deposit_C1()
            update_balance_C1()
            WS_COUNTERS.ws_tran_count += 1
        WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def validate_deposit_C1() -> None:
    """Validate deposit."""
    WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    if WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount < 0:
        WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD and ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_status != 'A':
        WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag = 'N'

def post_deposit_C1() -> None:
    """Post deposit."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_TOTALS
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD:
        ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance += WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount
        ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available += WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount
    WS_TOTALS.ws_total_deposits += WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount
    write_transaction_C1()

def update_balance_C1() -> None:
    """Update balance."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD:
        import datetime
        now = datetime.datetime.now()
        ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_last_trans_date = int(now.strftime("%Y%m%d"))
        #REWRITE account_record
def process_withdrawals_C1() -> None:
    """Process withdrawals."""
    print("PROCESSING WITHDRAWALS...")
    WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ account_master NEXT
        #AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #NOT AT END
        validate_withdrawal_C1()
        if WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
            post_withdrawal_C1()
            WS_COUNTERS.ws_tran_count += 1
        WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def validate_withdrawal_C1() -> None:
    """Validate withdrawal."""
    WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD and WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount > ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available:
        if ACCOUNT_RECORD and WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount > (ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available + ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_overdraft_limit):
            WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        else:
            apply_overdraft_fee_C1()

def apply_overdraft_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Apply overdraft fee."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_TOTALS, WS_FEE_SCHEDULE
    WS_TOTALS.ws_total_fees += WS_FEE_SCHEDULE.ws_overdraft_fee
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD:
        ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance -= WS_FEE_SCHEDULE.ws_overdraft_fee

def post_withdrawal_C1() -> None:
    """Post withdrawal."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_TOTALS
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD:
        ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance -= WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount
        ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available -= WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount
    WS_TOTALS.ws_total_withdrawals += WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_amount
    write_transaction_C1()

    """Wire transfer."""

def calculate_interest_C1() -> None:
    """Calculate interest."""
    print("CALCULATING INTEREST...")
    WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ account_master NEXT
        #AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #NOT AT END
        determine_rate_C1()
        compute_interest_C1()
        post_interest_C1()
        WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def determine_rate_C1() -> None:
    """Determine rate."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS, WS_INTEREST_RATES
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD:
        if ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == 'CH':
            WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_rate = WS_INTEREST_RATES.ws_checking_rate
        elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == 'SV':
            WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_rate = WS_INTEREST_RATES.ws_savings_rate
        elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == 'MM':
            WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_rate = WS_INTEREST_RATES.ws_mm_rate
        elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == 'CD':
            WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_rate = WS_INTEREST_RATES.ws_cd_rate_1yr
        else:
            WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_rate = Decimal('0')

def compute_interest_C1() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD:
        WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_interest = ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance * WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_rate / 12

def post_interest_C1() -> None:
    """Post interest."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_TOTALS, WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD:
        ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance += WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_interest
    WS_TOTALS.ws_total_interest += WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_interest

def apply_fees_C1() -> None:
    """Apply monthly fees."""
    print("APPLYING MONTHLY FEES...")
    WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ account_master NEXT
        #AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #NOT AT END
        check_minimum_balance_C1()
        if WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
            waive_fee_C1()
        else:
            charge_fee_C1()
        WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def check_minimum_balance_C1() -> None:
    """Check minimum balance."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD and ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance >= ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_min_balance:
        WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_FLAGS.ws_valid_flag = 'N'

def waive_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Waive fee."""
    pass

def charge_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Charge fee."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_TOTALS
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD:
        ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance -= ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_monthly_fee
        WS_TOTALS.ws_total_fees += ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_monthly_fee

def process_applications_C1() -> None:
    """Process loan applications."""
    print("PROCESSING LOAN APPLICATIONS...")
    pass

def process_payments_C1() -> None:
    """Process loan payments."""
    print("PROCESSING LOAN PAYMENTS...")
    WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ loan_master NEXT
        #AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #NOT AT END
        if LOAN_RECORD and LOAN_RECORD.loan_status == 'C':
            calculate_payment_C1()
            apply_payment_C1()
            update_loan_C1()
        WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def calculate_payment_C1() -> None:
    """Calculate payment."""
    global LOAN_RECORD, WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS
    if LOAN_RECORD:
        WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_payment = LOAN_RECORD.loan_payment_amount
        WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_interest = LOAN_RECORD.loan_current_balance * LOAN_RECORD.loan_interest_rate / 12
        WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_principal = WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_payment - WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_interest

def apply_payment_C1() -> None:
    """Apply payment."""
    global LOAN_RECORD, WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS, WS_TOTALS
    if LOAN_RECORD:
        LOAN_RECORD.loan_current_balance -= WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_principal
    WS_TOTALS.ws_total_payments += WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_payment
    WS_TOTALS.ws_total_interest += WS_CALCULATION_FIELDS.ws_calc_interest

def update_loan_C1() -> None:
    """Update loan."""
    global LOAN_RECORD
    if LOAN_RECORD:
        if LOAN_RECORD.loan_current_balance <= 0:
            LOAN_RECORD.loan_status = 'P'
        #REWRITE loan_record
def calculate_amortization_C1() -> None:
    """Calculate amortization schedules."""
    print("CALCULATING AMORTIZATION SCHEDULES...")
    pass

def assess_delinquencies_C1() -> None:
    """Assess delinquent loans."""
    print("ASSESSING DELINQUENT LOANS...")
    WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ loan_master NEXT
        #AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #NOT AT END
        check_payment_status_C1()
        if WS_FLAGS.ws_found_flag == 'N':
            mark_delinquent_C1()
            assess_late_fee_C1()
        WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def check_payment_status_C1() -> None:
    """Check payment status."""
    global LOAN_RECORD
    if LOAN_RECORD and LOAN_RECORD.loan_next_payment_date < WS_CURRENT_DATE_DATA.ws_current_date:
        WS_FLAGS.ws_found_flag = 'N'

def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates insurance premiums."""
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        insurance_master_record = insurance_master_next()
        if insurance_master_record is None:
            ws_eof = True
        else:
            determine_base_premium_C2()
            apply_risk_factor_C2()
            calculate_final_premium_C2()


def determine_base_premium_C2() -> None:
    """Determines the base premium based on insurance type."""
    if ins_life:
        ws_calc_amount = ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_life_rate_per_1000
    elif ins_health:
        ws_calc_amount = ws_health_base_premium
    elif ins_auto:
        ws_calc_amount = ws_auto_base_premium
    elif ins_home:
        ws_calc_amount = ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_home_rate_per_1000
    elif ins_umbrella:
        ws_calc_amount = ws_umbrella_rate


def apply_risk_factor_C2() -> None:
    """Applies a risk factor to the calculated amount."""
    if ins_claims_count > 2:
        ws_calc_amount = ws_calc_amount * Decimal('1.25')


def calculate_final_premium_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates and stores the final premium."""
    ins_premium_amount = ws_calc_amount
    global ws_total_premiums
    ws_total_premiums += ws_calc_amount

def calculate_portfolio_value_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates portfolio values."""
    print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        investment_master_record = investment_master_next()
        if investment_master_record is None:
            ws_eof = True
        else:
            calculate_position_value_C2()
            calculate_gain_loss_C2()
            update_totals_C2()


def calculate_position_value_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates the market value of an investment position."""
    inv_market_value = inv_quantity * inv_current_price


def calculate_gain_loss_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates the gain or loss on an investment."""
    inv_gain_loss = inv_market_value - (inv_quantity * inv_purchase_price)


def update_totals_C2() -> None:
    """Updates the total investment value."""
    global ws_total_investments
    ws_total_investments += inv_market_value

    """Calculates dividends for investments."""
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        investment_master_record = investment_master_next()

            if inv_dividend_rate > 0:
                compute_dividend_C2()
                post_dividend_C2()


def compute_dividend_C2() -> None:
    """Computes the dividend amount."""
    ws_calc_amount = inv_market_value * inv_dividend_rate / 4


def post_dividend_C2() -> None:
    """Posts the dividend to the total dividends."""
    global ws_total_dividends
    ws_total_dividends += ws_calc_amount

def validate_account_C2() -> None:
    """Validates an account."""
    ws_valid = True
    if acct_id == " " * len(acct_id):
        ws_invalid = True


def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates tax based on income brackets."""
    if ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_1_max:
        ws_calc_tax = ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_1_rate
    elif ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_2_max:
        ws_calc_tax = ((ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) + 0  # TODO
                       ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_1_max) * 0  # TODO
                        ws_bracket_2_rate))
    elif ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_3_max:
        ws_calc_tax = ((ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) + 0  # TODO
                       ((ws_bracket_2_max - ws_bracket_1_max) * 0  # TODO
                        ws_bracket_2_rate) + 0  # TODO
                       ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_2_max) * 0  # TODO
                        ws_bracket_3_rate))
    else:
        ws_calc_tax = ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_5_rate

def analyze_patterns_C2() -> None:
    """Analyzes transaction patterns for fraud."""
    print("ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        transaction_log_record = transaction_log_next()
        if transaction_log_record is None:
            ws_eof = True
        else:
            check_amount_threshold_C2()
            check_frequency_C2()
            check_time_pattern_C2()


def check_amount_threshold_C2() -> None:
    """Checks if the transaction amount exceeds a threshold."""
    if tran_amount > 10000:
        flag_large_transaction_C2()


def flag_large_transaction_C2() -> None:
    """Flags a large transaction for further investigation."""
    global ws_process_count
    ws_process_count += 1
    write_audit_C2()

    """Checks transaction frequency for suspicious patterns."""

def behavioral_scoring_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates behavioral scores for customers."""
    print("CALCULATING BEHAVIORAL SCORES...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        customer_master_record = customer_master_next()
        if customer_master_record is None:
            ws_eof = True
        else:
            calculate_risk_score_C2()
            update_customer_profile_C2()


def calculate_risk_score_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates a risk score based on customer data."""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = 0
    if cust_credit_score < 600:
        ws_calc_result += 30
    if cust_total_loans > cust_total_balance:
        ws_calc_result += 20


def update_customer_profile_C2() -> None:
    """Updates the customer profile with the calculated risk rating."""
    if ws_calc_result > 50:
        cust_risk_rating = 'H'
    elif ws_calc_result > 25:
        cust_risk_rating = 'M'
    else:
        cust_risk_rating = 'L'

    """Checks if the transaction exceeds the credit limit."""
    if ws_calc_amount > acct_overdraft_limit:
        ws_not_approved = True
    else:
        ws_approved = True

    """Checks the fraud score for the transaction."""
    pass

    """Calculates rewards points for the transaction."""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = tran_amount * Decimal('0.01')
    global ws_total_fees
    ws_total_fees += ws_calc_result

    """Applies interest to the credit card balance."""
    global ws_calc_interest
    ws_calc_interest = acct_balance * ws_credit_card_rate / 12
    global acct_balance
    acct_balance += ws_calc_interest

def dti_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates Debt-to-Income (DTI) ratio."""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = (loan_payment_amount / 0  # TODO
                      (cust_total_balance / 12))
    if ws_calc_result > Decimal('0.43'):
        ws_not_approved = True


def ltv_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates Loan-to-Value (LTV) ratio."""
    loan_ltv_ratio = (loan_current_balance / 0  # TODO
                       loan_collateral_value)
    if loan_ltv_ratio > Decimal('0.80'):
        global ws_calc_fee
        ws_calc_fee += ws_loan_origination_pct


def credit_analysis_C2() -> None:
    """Analyzes the creditworthiness of the applicant."""
    if cust_credit_score < 620:
        ws_not_approved = True

        ws_calc_result = ((inv_current_price - inv_purchase_price) / 0  # TODO
                          inv_purchase_price * 100)

        ws_temp_flag = 'M'

        ws_temp_flag = 'M'

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_not_eof_C3, ws_eof_C3
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    ws_not_eof_C3 = True
    while not ws_eof_C3:
        # Assuming customer_master is a file/collection that can be read
        try:
            customer = next(customer_master_C3) # type: ignore # Assuming customer_master_C3 is an iterator
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_C3 = True

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_result_C3, cust_total_balance_C3, ws_savings_rate_C3, cust_total_loans_C3, ws_personal_rate_C3, cust_total_investments_C3
    ws_calc_result_C3 = (cust_total_balance_C3 * ws_savings_rate_C3) + (cust_total_loans_C3 * ws_personal_rate_C3) + (cust_total_investments_C3 * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_result_C3, ws_temp_code_C3
    if ws_calc_result_C3 > 10000:
        ws_temp_code_C3 = 'PLATINUM'
    elif ws_calc_result_C3 > 5000:
        ws_temp_code_C3 = 'GOLD'
    elif ws_calc_result_C3 > 1000:
        ws_temp_code_C3 = 'SILVER'
    else:
        ws_temp_code_C3 = 'BRONZE'

    """Brief description."""

        ws_calc_result_C3 += 25
    if cust_credit_score_C3 < 600:
        ws_calc_result_C3 += 30

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global acct_balance_C3, acct_min_balance_C3, ws_calc_amount_C3, ws_total_investments_C3
    if acct_balance_C3 > acct_min_balance_C3:
        ws_calc_amount_C3 = acct_balance_C3 - acct_min_balance_C3
        acct_balance_C3 -= ws_calc_amount_C3
        ws_total_investments_C3 += ws_calc_amount_C3

def extract_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_not_eof_C3, ws_eof_C3, ws_process_count_C3
    ws_not_eof_C3 = True
    while not ws_eof_C3:
        try:
            customer = next(customer_master_C3) # type: ignore # Assuming customer_master_C3 is an iterator
            ws_process_count_C3 += 1
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_C3 = True

@dataclass
class Customer:
    cust_id: str

    cust_total_loans: Decimal
    cust_total_investments: Decimal
    cust_last_activity: int

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """Monitoring transactions."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    working_storage_C4.ws_not_eof = True
    working_storage_C4.ws_eof = False
    while not working_storage_C4.ws_eof:
        # Simulate reading transaction log
        transaction = {"amount": 12000}
        transaction_log_C4.tran_amount = transaction["amount"]
        if transaction_log_C4.tran_amount is None:
            working_storage_C4.ws_eof = True
        else:
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
            c130_network_analysis_C4()
            working_storage_C4.ws_eof = True

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """Rule-based detection."""
    if transaction_log_C4.tran_amount >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4()
    if 5000 <= transaction_log_C4.tran_amount < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4()

def c111_flag_ctr_C4() -> None:
    """Flag CTR."""
    working_storage_C4.ws_process_count += 1

def c112_check_structuring_C4() -> None:

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """Classification."""
    if data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 750:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 650:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 550:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """Regression."""
    data_record_C4.cust_credit_score = data_record_C4.cust_credit_score or 0
    data_record_C4.cust_total_balance = data_record_C4.cust_total_balance or Decimal('0')
    data_record_C4.cust_total_loans = data_record_C4.cust_total_loans or Decimal('0')
    working_storage_C4.ws_calc_result = (data_record_C4.cust_credit_score * 10) + \
                                        (data_record_C4.cust_total_balance / 1000) - \
                                        (data_record_C4.cust_total_loans / 2000)

def initialization_1000_C5():
    """Initialization."""

def open_files_1100_C5():
    """Opens files."""
    global WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_FILE_STATUS: Any = None
    WS_ERROR_MSG: Any = None
    try:
        customer_file = open("customer_file", "r")
        account_file = open("account_file", "r")
        transaction_file = open("transaction_file", "r")
        report_file = open("report_file", "w")
        error_file = open("error_file", "w")
        master_file = open("master_file", "r+")

        customer_file.close()
        account_file.close()
        transaction_file.close()
        report_file.close()
        error_file.close()
        master_file.close()

        WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'
    except Exception as e:
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '99'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def read_parameters_1200_C5():
    """Reads parameters."""
    global WS_PARAM_DATE, WS_PARAM_TIME, WS_JOB_ID, WS_ENV_TYPE, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    import datetime
    WS_PARAM_DATE = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_PARAM_TIME = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
    WS_JOB_ID = 'batch_001'
    WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
    WS_PROCESS_DATE = int(WS_PARAM_DATE)

def initialize_tables_1300_C5():
    """Initializes tables."""
    global RATE_TABLE_ENTRY, BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY
    RATE_TABLE_ENTRY: List[Any] = [None] * 100
    BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY: List[Any] = [None] * 50
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_idx_1] = None
        RT_RATE: Any = Decimal('0')
        RT_CODE: Any = ' ' * 10
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):
        BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_idx_1] = None

def load_reference_data_1400_C5():
    """Loads reference data."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_REF_RECORD, RT_CODE, RT_RATE, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    REFERENCE_FILE = ["CODE1,1.23", "CODE2,4.56"]
    WS_TBL_IDX: int = 1
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
        try:
            WS_REF_RECORD = REFERENCE_FILE[WS_TBL_idx_1]
            WS_REF_CODE, WS_REF_RATE = WS_REF_RECORD.split(",")
            RT_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_CODE
            RT_RATE = Decimal(WS_REF_RATE)
            if len(RATE_TABLE_ENTRY) < WS_TBL_IDX:
                RATE_TABLE_ENTRY.append(None)
            RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_idx_1] = {"RT_CODE": RT_CODE, "RT_RATE": RT_RATE}
            WS_TBL_IDX += 1
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def process_transactions_2000_C5():
    """Processes transactions."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_TRANSACTION_REC
    WS_TRANSACTION_REC = {"TXN_ACCOUNT_ID": "12345", "TXN_AMOUNT": "100", "TXN_TYPE": "D"}
    try:
        WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1
        validate_transaction_2100_C5()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
            process_by_type_2200_C5()
        else:
            handle_error_2900_C5()
    except EOFError:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def validate_transaction_2100_C5():
    """Validates transaction."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    TXN_ACCOUNT_ID: Any = WS_TRANSACTION_REC["TXN_ACCOUNT_ID"]
    TXN_AMOUNT: Any = WS_TRANSACTION_REC["TXN_AMOUNT"]
    TXN_TYPE: Any = WS_TRANSACTION_REC["TXN_TYPE"]
    if not TXN_ACCOUNT_ID or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID.isspace():
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return
    try:
        float(TXN_AMOUNT)
    except ValueError:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return
    if TXN_TYPE not in ['D', 'W', 'T', 'I']:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'
    validate_account_exists_2150_C5()
    validate_business_rules_2160_C5()

def validate_account_exists_2150_C5():
    """Validates account exists."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def validate_business_rules_2160_C5():
    """Validates business rules."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_TYPE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    if TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        if float(TXN_AMOUNT) > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if float(TXN_AMOUNT) > 1000000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

def process_by_type_2200_C5():
    """Processes transaction by type."""
    global TXN_TYPE
    if TXN_TYPE == 'D':
        process_deposit_2300_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        process_withdrawal_2400_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'T':
        process_transfer_2500_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'I':
        process_interest_2600_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def process_deposit_2300_C5():
    """Processes deposit."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT)
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT)
    WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def update_account_2350_C5():
    """Updates account."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_LAST_UPDATE, WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    ACCT_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    import datetime
    ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'

def write_audit_trail_2380_C5():
    """Writes audit trail."""
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE, WS_JOB_ID
    import datetime
    AUDIT_TIMESTAMP: Any = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD = {"AUDIT_ACCOUNT":TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, "AUDIT_AMOUNT":TXN_AMOUNT, "AUDIT_TYPE":TXN_TYPE, "AUDIT_TIMESTAMP":AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, "AUDIT_JOB_ID":WS_JOB_ID}

def process_withdrawal_2400_C5():
    """Processes withdrawal."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS, WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT, WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT)
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'WITHDRAWAL'
    WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT)
    WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE < WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT:
        generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5()

def generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5():
    """Generates low balance alert."""
    global WS_ALERT_RECORD, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_ALERT_COUNT
    import datetime
    ALERT_DATE: Any = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_ALERT_RECORD = {"ALERT_TYPE": 'low_bal', "ALERT_ACCOUNT": TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, "ALERT_BALANCE": WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, "ALERT_DATE": ALERT_DATE}
    WS_ALERT_COUNT += 1

def process_transfer_2500_C5():
    """Processes transfer."""
    validate_target_account_2510_C5()
    if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
        debit_source_2520_C5()

def validate_target_account_2510_C5():

    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':

def debit_source_2520_C5():
    """Debits source account."""
    global WS_SOURCE_BALANCE, TXN_AMOUNT, ACCT_BALANCE
    WS_SOURCE_BALANCE -= Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT)
    ACCT_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was WS_SOURCE_BALANCE

def credit_target_2530_C5():
    """Credits target account."""
    global WS_TARGET_BALANCE, TXN_AMOUNT, ACCT_ID
    WS_TARGET_BALANCE += Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT)
    ACCT_ID  = None  # TODO: was TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_REC = {"ACCT_ID": ACCT_ID, "ACCT_BALANCE": WS_TARGET_BALANCE}

def record_transfer_2540_C5():
    """Records transfer."""
    global TXN_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS, WS_TRANSFER_COUNT
    WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS += Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT)
    WS_TRANSFER_COUNT += 1
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def process_interest_2600_C5():
    """Processes interest."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_INTEREST_RATE, WS_TOTAL_INTEREST, WS_INTEREST_COUNT
    WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * WS_INTEREST_RATE / 100
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'INTEREST'
    WS_TOTAL_INTEREST += None  # TODO: was WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT
    WS_INTEREST_COUNT += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def handle_error_2900_C5():
    """Handles error."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT, WS_ERROR_RECORD, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_MAX_ERRORS, WS_ABORT_REASON
    WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1
    import datetime
    ERR_TIMESTAMP: Any = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_ERROR_RECORD = {"ERR_ACCOUNT":TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, "ERR_MESSAGE":WS_ERROR_MSG, "ERR_TIMESTAMP":ERR_TIMESTAMP}
    if WS_ERROR_COUNT > WS_MAX_ERRORS:
        WS_ABORT_REASON = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def batch_processing_3000_C5():
    """Processes a batch of transactions."""
    load_batch_header_3100_C5()
    while WS_BATCH_EOF != 'Y':
        process_batch_items_3200_C5()
    validate_batch_totals_3300_C5()
    commit_batch_3400_C5()

def load_batch_header_3100_C5():
    """Loads the batch header."""
    global WS_BATCH_EOF, WS_CURRENT_BATCH, WS_EXPECTED_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL
    try:
        BATCH_FILE = [{"BATCH_ID": "B1", "BATCH_COUNT": "3", "BATCH_TOTAL": "300"}, {"ITEM_ACCOUNT": "A1", "ITEM_AMOUNT": "100", "ITEM_TYPE": "PAY"}]
        WS_BATCH_HEADER = BATCH_FILE[0]
        WS_CURRENT_BATCH = WS_BATCH_HEADER["BATCH_ID"]
        WS_EXPECTED_COUNT = int(WS_BATCH_HEADER["BATCH_COUNT"])
        WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL = Decimal(WS_BATCH_HEADER["BATCH_TOTAL"])
    except IndexError:
        WS_BATCH_EOF = 'Y'

def process_batch_items_3200_C5():
    """Processes batch items."""
    global WS_BATCH_EOF, WS_ACTUAL_COUNT, WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL, BATCH_FILE, WS_ITEM_AMOUNT
    try:
        ITEM_AMOUNT: Any = 100
        WS_BATCH_ITEM: Any = {"ITEM_AMOUNT":ITEM_AMOUNT, "ITEM_TYPE":"PAY", "ITEM_ACCOUNT":"A1"}
        WS_ACTUAL_COUNT += 1
        WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL += Decimal(ITEM_AMOUNT)
        process_single_item_3250_C5()
    except (IndexError, NameError):
        WS_BATCH_EOF = 'Y'

def process_single_item_3250_C5():
    """Processes a single item."""
    global ITEM_TYPE, WS_BATCH_ITEM
    ITEM_TYPE = WS_BATCH_ITEM["ITEM_TYPE"]

    if ITEM_TYPE == 'PAY':
        process_payment_3260_C5()
    elif ITEM_TYPE == 'REF':
        process_refund_3270_C5()
    elif ITEM_TYPE == 'ADJ':
        process_adjustment_3280_C5()

def process_payment_3260_C5():
    """Processes a payment."""
    global ITEM_ACCOUNT, WS_BATCH_ITEM, WS_PAYMENT_COUNT
    ITEM_ACCOUNT = WS_BATCH_ITEM["ITEM_ACCOUNT"]
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ITEM_ACCOUNT
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        subtract_from_balance_C5()
        add_payment_count_C5()

def process_refund_3270_C5():
    """Processes a refund."""
    global ITEM_ACCOUNT, WS_BATCH_ITEM, WS_REFUND_COUNT
    ITEM_ACCOUNT = WS_BATCH_ITEM["ITEM_ACCOUNT"]

    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':

def process_adjustment_3280_C5():
    """Processes an adjustment."""
    global ITEM_ACCOUNT, WS_BATCH_ITEM, WS_ADJUSTMENT_COUNT, WS_ITEM_AMOUNT
    ITEM_ACCOUNT = WS_BATCH_ITEM["ITEM_ACCOUNT"]
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ITEM_ACCOUNT
    search_account_5000_C5()
    WS_ITEM_AMOUNT: int = 10
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ITEM_AMOUNT > 0:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_ITEM_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_ITEM_AMOUNT

        WS_ADJUSTMENT_COUNT += 1

def validate_batch_totals_3300_C5():
    """Validates batch totals."""
    global WS_ACTUAL_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_COUNT, WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL, WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL, WS_ERROR_MSG
    if WS_ACTUAL_COUNT != WS_EXPECTED_COUNT:
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'BATCH COUNT MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_3350_C5()
    if WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL != WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL:
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'BATCH TOTAL MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_3350_C5()

def reject_batch_3350_C5():
    """Rejects the batch."""
    global WS_CURRENT_BATCH, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_REJECTED_BATCH_COUNT
    import datetime
    REJ_DATE: Any = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_REJECTION_RECORD = {"REJ_BATCH_ID": WS_CURRENT_BATCH, "REJ_REASON": WS_ERROR_MSG, "REJ_DATE": REJ_DATE}
    WS_REJECTED_BATCH_COUNT += 1

def commit_batch_3400_C5():
    """Commits the batch."""
    global WS_BATCH_VALID, WS_COMMITTED_BATCH_COUNT
    WS_BATCH_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_BATCH_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_COMMITTED_BATCH_COUNT += 1
        update_batch_status_3450_C5()

def update_batch_status_3450_C5():
    """Updates the batch status."""
    global BATCH_COMMIT_DATE
    import datetime
    BATCH_STATUS: Any = 'COMMITTED'
    BATCH_COMMIT_DATE: Any = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")

def search_account_5000_C5():
    """Searches for an account."""
    global WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_SEARCH_KEY, ACCT_ID, WS_ACCOUNT_REC, ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_TYPE, ACCT_STATUS, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE, WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS
    WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'N'
    ACCT_ID  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_KEY
    # Simulate reading a file.  Replace with actual file read
    account_data = {"12345": {"ACCT_BALANCE": 1000.00, "ACCT_TYPE": "CHECKING", "ACCT_STATUS": "ACTIVE"}}

    if ACCT_ID in account_data:
        WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'Y'
        ACCT_BALANCE = account_data[ACCT_ID]["ACCT_BALANCE"]
        ACCT_TYPE = account_data[ACCT_ID]["ACCT_TYPE"]
        ACCT_STATUS = account_data[ACCT_ID]["ACCT_STATUS"]

        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_BALANCE
        WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_TYPE
        WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_STATUS

def binary_search_5100_C5():
    """Performs a binary search."""
    global WS_LOW, WS_HIGH, WS_TABLE_SIZE, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_MID, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_INDEX, TBL_KEY

    WS_LOW = 1
    WS_HIGH  = None  # TODO: was WS_TABLE_SIZE
    WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_LOW <= WS_HIGH:
        WS_MID = (WS_LOW + WS_HIGH) // 2
        if TBL_KEY[WS_mid_1] == WS_SEARCH_KEY:
            WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'Y'
            WS_FOUND_INDEX  = None  # TODO: was WS_MID
            break
        elif TBL_KEY[WS_mid_1] < WS_SEARCH_KEY:
            WS_LOW = WS_MID + 1
        else:
            WS_HIGH = WS_MID - 1

def hash_lookup_5200_C5():
    """Performs a hash lookup."""
    global WS_HASH_VALUE, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_HASH_TABLE_SIZE, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_LOOKUP_RESULT, HASH_KEY, HASH_VALUE

    WS_HASH_VALUE = ord(WS_SEARCH_KEY[0]) * 31 + ord(WS_SEARCH_KEY[1]) % WS_HASH_TABLE_SIZE
    WS_HASH_VALUE += 1

    if HASH_KEY[WS_HASH_value_1] == WS_SEARCH_KEY:
        WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_LOOKUP_RESULT = HASH_VALUE[WS_HASH_value_1]
    else:
        probe

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculate PMI amount."""
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """Evaluate payment history and adjust risk score."""
    ws_factor_1 = ""
    ws_factor_2 = ""
    ws_factor_3 = ""
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """Calculate final risk score and category."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    ws_risk_category = ""
    if ws_risk_score >= 80:
        ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 60:
        ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 40:
        ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
    else:
        ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Determine loan approval status."""
    ws_approval_status = ""
    ws_conditions = ""
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal(0)
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount, ws_base_rate, ws_credit_tier, ws_risk_category)
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculate approved loan terms."""
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal(0)
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')
    return ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Generate loan terms."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_loan_principal_bal

def calc_auto_premium_C8(ws_driver_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates auto premium."""
    if 6 <= ws_driver_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium += 100
    else:
        ws_base_premium += 50
    if ws_driver_age < 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        ws_accident_surcharge = ws_accidents_3yr * 200
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge
    if ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        ws_violation_surcharge = ws_violations_3yr * 100
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_home_premium_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates home premium."""
    ws_base_premium = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.003')
    if 0 <= ws_home_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    elif 11 <= ws_home_age <= 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 26 <= ws_home_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.2')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_security_system == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    ws_deductible_credit = ws_deductible / 1000 * 50
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit
    if ws_base_premium < 200:
        ws_base_premium = Decimal('200')
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_health_premium_C8(ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_insured_age: int, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates health premium."""
    ws_base_premium = Decimal('300')
    if 0 <= ws_insured_age <= 18:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.5')
    elif 19 <= ws_insured_age <= 30:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 31 <= ws_insured_age <= 40:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif 41 <= ws_insured_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    elif 51 <= ws_insured_age <= 60:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.0')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.8')
    if ws_plan_type == 'BRONZE':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.8')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'SILVER':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'GOLD':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'PLATINUM':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    if ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.5')
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium = ws_monthly_premium * 12

def underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """Performs underwriting."""
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    check_medical_history_C8()
    verify_information_C8()
    determine_decision_C8()

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life: bool, ws_bmi: Decimal, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, policy_auto: bool, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Evaluates risk factors."""
    ws_risk_points = 0
    if policy_life:
        if ws_bmi > 30:
            ws_risk_points += 10
        if ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 25
        if ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 15
    if policy_auto:
        if ws_driver_age < 21:
            ws_risk_points += 20
        if ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            ws_risk_points += 15

def check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_risk_points: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_condition_points: int) -> None:
    """Checks medical history."""
    if ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        ws_condition_points = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points
    if ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    if ws_prescription_count > 5:
        ws_risk_points += 5

def verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """Verifies information."""
    check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    validate_documents_C8()

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str, ws_address_mismatch: str) -> None:
    """Checks fraud indicators."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 3:
        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10

def validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Validates documents."""
    if ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points: int, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determines decision."""
    if ws_risk_points > 50:
        ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
    elif ws_risk_points > 30:
        ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    elif ws_risk_points > 15:
        ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
    else:
        ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('0.9')

def issue_policy_C8(ws_uw_decision: str) -> None:
    """Issues policy."""
    if ws_uw_decision != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number_C8()
        create_policy_record_C8()
        set_beneficiaries_C8()
        send_policy_docs_C8()
    else:
        send_decline_letter_C8()

def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: int, schedule_type: str) -> None:
    """Calculates the next run date based on the schedule type."""
    if schedule_type == 'DAILY':
        ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 1
    elif schedule_type == 'WEEKLY':
        ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 7
    elif schedule_type == 'MONTHLY':
        ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 30
    elif schedule_type == 'QUARTERLY':
        ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 90
    elif schedule_type == 'YEARLY':
        ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 365

def data_analytics_C10() -> None:
    """Performs data analytics and reporting procedures."""
    collect_metrics_C10()
    aggregate_data_C10()
    calculate_kpi_C10()
    generate_dashboard_C10()
    export_data_C10()

def collect_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects various metrics."""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects transaction-related metrics."""
    ws_total_trans_amount = 0
    ws_total_trans_count = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = read_transaction_file()
            ws_total_trans_count += 1
            ws_total_trans_amount += ws_trans_rec.trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects customer-related metrics."""
    ws_active_customers = 0
    ws_new_customers = 0
    ws_churned_customers = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = read_customer_file()
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_status == 'A':
                ws_active_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_open_date >= ws_period_start:
                ws_new_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_close_date >= ws_period_start:
                ws_churned_customers += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_performance_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects performance-related metrics."""
    ws_response_time_total = 0
    ws_response_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = read_perf_log_file()
            ws_response_time_total += ws_perf_rec.perf_response_time
            ws_response_count += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_response_count > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def aggregate_data_C10() -> None:
    """Aggregates collected data."""
    daily_aggregation_C10()
    weekly_aggregation_C10()
    monthly_aggregation_C10()

def daily_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs daily data aggregation."""
    ws_daily_summary = DailySummary()
    ws_daily_summary.daily_date = ws_process_date
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount
    ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits = ws_total_deposits
    ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = ws_total_withdrawals
    write_daily_summary_record(ws_daily_summary)

def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs weekly data aggregation."""
    if ws_day_of_week == 7:
        ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummary()
        ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week = ws_week_number
        sum_week_data_C10(ws_weekly_summary)
        write_weekly_summary_record(ws_weekly_summary)

def sum_week_data_C10(weekly_summary: "WeeklySummary") -> None:
    """Sums data for the week."""
    weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count = 0
    weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = 0
    for _ in range(7):
        try:
            daily_summary = read_daily_summary_record()
            weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count += daily_summary.daily_trans_count
            weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount += daily_summary.daily_trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            break

def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs monthly data aggregation."""
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummary()
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month = ws_curr_month
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year = ws_curr_year
        sum_month_data_C10(ws_monthly_summary)
        write_monthly_summary_record(ws_monthly_summary)

def sum_month_data_C10(monthly_summary: "MonthlySummary") -> None:
    """Sums data for the month."""
    monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = 0
    monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = 0
    monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = 0
    monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()
            if ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_month == ws_curr_month:
                monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates Key Performance Indicators (KPIs)."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()

def calc_financial_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates financial KPIs."""
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_roa = (ws_net_income / ws_total_assets) * 100
    if ws_total_equity > 0:
        ws_roe = (ws_net_income / ws_total_equity) * 100
    if ws_interest_expense > 0:
        ws_nim = ((ws_interest_income - ws_interest_expense) / ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates operational KPIs."""
    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_error_rate = (ws_error_count / ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    ws_sla_compliance = (ws_within_sla_count / ws_total_cases) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution = (ws_fcr_count / ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates customer KPIs."""
    if ws_active_customers > 0:
        ws_churn_rate = (ws_churned_customers / ws_active_customers) * 100
    ws_acquisition_cost = ws_marketing_spend / ws_new_customers
    ws_lifetime_value = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws_avg_customer_tenure

def generate_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Generates dashboards."""
    create_executive_dashboard_C10()
    create_operations_dashboard_C10()
    create_risk_dashboard_C10()

def create_executive_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates the executive dashboard."""
    ws_exec_dashboard = ExecutiveDashboard()
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_revenue = ws_total_revenue
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_net_income = ws_net_income
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roa = ws_roa
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roe = ws_roe
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_customers = ws_active_customers
    write_dashboard_record(ws_exec_dashboard)

def create_operations_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates the operations dashboard."""
    ws_ops_dashboard = OperationsDashboard()
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_title = 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD'
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_avg_response = ws_avg_response_time
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_error_rate = ws_error_rate
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_sla_pct = ws_sla_compliance
    write_dashboard_record(ws_ops_dashboard)

def create_risk_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates the risk dashboard."""
    ws_risk_dashboard = RiskDashboard()
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_title = 'RISK DASHBOARD'
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_fraud_score = ws_fraud_score
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_npl = ws_npl_ratio
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_capital = ws_capital_ratio
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_liquidity = ws_liquidity_ratio
    write_dashboard_record(ws_risk_dashboard)

def export_data_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data in various formats."""
    export_csv_C10()
    export_xml_C10()
    export_json_C10()

def export_csv_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to a CSV file."""
    with open('CSV_EXPORT_FILE', 'w') as csv_file:
        ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
        csv_file.write(ws_csv_header + '
')
        ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
            try:
                ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()
                ws_csv_line = f"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date},{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount},{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_deposits},{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_withdrawals}"
                csv_file.write(ws_csv_line + '
')
            except EOFError:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def export_xml_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to an XML file."""
    with open('XML_EXPORT_FILE', 'w') as xml_file:
        xml_file.write('<?xml version="1.0"?>
')
        xml_file.write('<DailySummaries>
')
        write_xml_records_C10(xml_file)
        xml_file.write('</DailySummaries>
')

def write_xml_records_C10(xml_file: "TextIOWrapper") -> None:
    """Writes XML records to the XML file."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()
            format_xml_record_C10(xml_file, ws_daily_sum_rec)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_xml_record_C10(xml_file: "TextIOWrapper", daily_sum_rec: "DailySummaryRecord") -> None:
    """Formats a single record as XML."""
    xml_file.write('<Summary>
')
    xml_file.write(f'<Date>{daily_sum_rec.daily_date}</Date>
')
    xml_file.write(f'<TransCount>{daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count}</TransCount>
')
    xml_file.write('</Summary>
')

def export_json_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to a JSON file."""
    with open('JSON_EXPORT_FILE', 'w') as json_file:
        json_file.write('{"dailySummaries":[
')
        write_json_records_C10(json_file)
        json_file.write(']}
')

def write_json_records_C10(json_file: "TextIOWrapper") -> None:
    """Writes JSON records to the JSON file."""
    ws_first_record = 'N'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()
            format_json_record_C10(json_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, ws_first_record)
            ws_first_record = 'Y'
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_json_record_C10(json_file: "TextIOWrapper", daily_sum_rec: "DailySummaryRecord", ws_first_record: str) -> None:
    """Formats a single record as JSON."""
    if ws_first_record == 'Y':
        ws_json_comma = ','
    else:
        ws_json_comma = ''
    json_line = f'{ws_json_comma}{{"date":"{daily_sum_rec.daily_date}","transCount":{daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},"transAmount":{daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount}}}'
    json_file.write(json_line + '
')

def account_maintenance_C10() -> None:
    """Performs account maintenance procedures."""
    dormant_account_check_C10()
    escheatment_processing_C10()
    account_closure_C10()
    account_reactivation_C10()

def dormant_account_check_C10() -> None:
    """Checks for dormant accounts."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = read_account_file()
            check_activity_C10(ws_account_rec)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_activity_C10(account_rec: "AccountRecord") -> None:
    """Checks account activity."""
    ws_days_inactive = integer_of_date(ws_process_date) - integer_of_date(account_rec.acct_last_activity)
    if ws_days_inactive > 365:
        account_rec.acct_status = 'D'
        mark_dormant_C10(account_rec)

def mark_dormant_C10(account_rec: "AccountRecord") -> None:
    """Marks an account as dormant."""
    account_rec.acct_status_desc = 'DORMANT'
    account_rec.acct_dormant_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_account_record(account_rec)
    send_dormant_notice_C10()

def send_dormant_notice_C10() -> None:
    """Sends a dormant account notice."""
    ws_notif_type = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification_C10()

def escheatment_processing_C10() -> None:
    """Processes accounts for escheatment."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = read_account_file()
            if ws_account_rec.acct_status == 'D':
                check_escheatment_C10(ws_account_rec)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_escheatment_C10(account_rec: "AccountRecord") -> None:
    """Checks if an account is eligible for escheatment."""
    ws_dormant_years = (integer_of_date(ws_process_date) - integer_of_date(account_rec.acct_dormant_date)) / 365
    if ws_dormant_years >= ws_escheat_years:
        escheat_account_C10(account_rec)

def escheat_account_C10(account_rec: "AccountRecord") -> None:
    """Escheats an account."""
    account_rec.acct_status = 'E'
    ws_escheat_amount = account_rec.acct_balance
    account_rec.acct_balance = 0
    create_escheat_record_C10(account_rec, ws_escheat_amount)
    rewrite_account_record(account_rec)

def create_escheat_record_C10(account_rec: "AccountRecord", escheat_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Creates an escheat record."""
    ws_escheat_record = EscheatRecord()
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_account = account_rec.acct_id
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_amount = escheat_amount
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_date = ws_process_date
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_owner = account_rec.acct_owner_name
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_address = account_rec.acct_owner_address
    write_escheat_record(ws_escheat_record)

def account_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes account closures."""
    if ws_close_request == 'Y':
        closure_validation = validate_closure_C10()
        if closure_validation.is_valid:
            process_closure_C10()
        else:
            reject_closure_C10(closure_validation.rejection_reason)

@dataclass
class ClosureValidationResult:
    is_valid: bool
    rejection_reason: Optional[str] = None

def validate_closure_C10() -> ClosureValidationResult:
    """Validates an account closure request."""
    if acct_balance < 0:
        return ClosureValidationResult(is_valid=False, rejection_reason='NEGATIVE BALANCE')
    if acct_pending_trans > 0:
        return ClosureValidationResult(is_valid=False, rejection_reason='PENDING TRANSACTIONS')
    if acct_loan_link != '':
        return ClosureValidationResult(is_valid=False, rejection_reason='LINKED LOAN EXISTS')
    return ClosureValidationResult(is_valid=True)

def process_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes an account closure."""
    ws_final_balance = acct_balance
    disburse_balance_C10(ws_final_balance)
    acct_status = 'C'
    acct_close_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_account_record(acct_record)
    archive_account_C10()

def disburse_balance_C10(final_balance: Decimal) -> None:
    """Disburses the account balance."""
    if final_balance > 0:
        ws_check_record = CheckRecord()
        ws_check_record.check_from_account = acct_id
        ws_check_record.check_amount = final_balance
        ws_check_record.check_memo = 'ACCOUNT CLOSURE'
        ws_check_record.check_payee = acct_owner_name
        write_check_record(ws_check_record)

def archive_account_C10() -> None:
    """Archives the closed account."""
    ws_archive_record = ArchiveRecord()
    ws_archive_record.archive_account_data = ws_account_rec
    ws_archive_record.archive_date = ws_process_date
    ws_archive_record.archive_retention = integer_of_date(ws_process_date) + 2555
    write_archive_record(ws_archive_record)

def reject_closure_C10(rejection_reason: str) -> None:
    """Rejects an account closure request."""
    ws_notif_type = 'closure_reject'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Closure rejected: {rejection_reason}'
    send_notification_C10()

def account_reactivation_C10() -> None:
    """Processes account reactivations."""
    if ws_reactivate_request == 'Y':
        reactivation_validation = validate_reactivation_C10()
        if reactivation_validation.is_valid:
            process_reactivation_C10()

@dataclass
class ReactivationValidationResult:
    is_valid: bool
    rejection_reason: Optional[str] = None

def validate_reactivation_C10() -> ReactivationValidationResult:
    """Validates an account reactivation request."""
    if acct_status == 'E':
        return ReactivationValidationResult(is_valid=False, rejection_reason='ACCOUNT ESCHEATED')
    if acct_status == 'C':
        if ws_days_since_close > 90:
            return ReactivationValidationResult(is_valid=False, rejection_reason='CLOSURE PERIOD EXCEEDED')
    return ReactivationValidationResult(is_valid=True)

def process_reactivation_C10() -> None:
    """Processes an account reactivation."""
    acct_status = 'A'
    acct_react_date = ws_process_date
    acct_dormant_date = ''
    rewrite_account_record(acct_record)
    send_reactivation_confirm_C10()

def send_reactivation_confirm_C10() -> None:
    """Sends an account reactivation confirmation."""
    ws_notif_type = 'REACTIVATION'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Your account has been reactivated'
    send_notification_C10()

def card_management_C10() -> None:
    """Performs card management procedures."""
    card_issuance_C10()
    card_activation_C10()
    pin_management_C10()
    card_replacement_C10()
    card_blocking_C10()

def card_issuance_C10() -> None:
    """Handles card issuance procedures."""
    generate_card_number_C10()
    set_card_limits_C10()
    assign_network_C10()
    create_card_record_C10()

def generate_card_number_C10() -> None:
    """Generates a card number."""
    ws_card_prefix = '4'
    ws_card_bin = ws_bin_number
    ws_card_seq = int(random.random() * 999999999)
    ws_card_number_temp = f'{ws_card_prefix}{ws_card_bin}{ws_card_seq}'
    ws_luhn_check = calculate_luhn_check_C10(ws_card_number_temp)
    ws_card_number = f'{ws_card_number_temp}{ws_luhn_check}'

def calculate_luhn_check_C10(card_number_temp: str) -> int:
    """Calculates the Luhn check digit."""
    ws_luhn_sum = 0
    for ws_luhn_idx in range(15, 0, -1):
        ws_luhn_digit = int(card_number_temp[ws_luhn_idx-1])
        if (16 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9
        ws_luhn_sum += ws_luhn_digit
    return (10 - (ws_luhn_sum % 10)) % 10

def set_card_limits_C10() -> None:
    """Sets card limits based on card type."""
    if ws_card_type == 'DEBIT':
        ws_daily_limit = 1000
        ws_atm_limit = 500
    elif ws_card_type == 'CREDIT':
        ws_daily_limit = ws_credit_line
        ws_atm_limit = ws_credit_line * 0.2
    elif ws_card_type == 'PREMIUM':
        ws_daily_limit = 10000
        ws_atm_limit = 2000

def assign_network_C10() -> None:
    """Assigns a card network based on the card prefix."""
    if ws_card_prefix == '4':
        ws_card_network = 'VISA'
    elif ws_card_prefix == '5':
        ws_card_network = 'MASTERCARD'
    elif ws_card_prefix == '3':
        ws_card_network = 'AMEX'
    else:
        ws_card_network = 'DISCOVER'

def create_card_record_C10() -> None:
    """Creates a card record."""
    ws_card_record = CardRecord()
    ws_card_record.card_number = ws_card_number
    ws_card_record.card_type = ws_card_type
    ws_card_record.card_network = ws_card_network
    ws_card_record.card_daily_limit = ws_daily_limit
    ws_card_record.card_atm_limit = ws_atm_limit
    ws_card_record.card_expiry_date = integer_of_date(ws_process_date) + 1095
    ws_card_record.card_status = 'I'
    write_card_record(ws_card_record)

def card_activation_C10() -> None:
    """Handles card activation procedures."""
    if ws_activation_request == 'Y':
        verification_result = verify_cardholder_C10()
        if verification_result:
            activate_card_C10()
        else:
            activation_failed_C10()

def verify_cardholder_C10() -> bool:
    """Verifies the cardholder."""
    if ws_cvv_input == ws_card_cvv and ws_dob_input == ws_cardholder_dob and ws_ssn_last4_input == ws_cardholder_ssn_last4:
        return True
    return False

def activate_card_C10() -> None:
    """Activates the card."""
    card_status = 'A'
    card_activation_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_card_record(card_record)
    ws_notif_type = 'card_activated'
    ws_notif_channel = 'SMS'
    ws_notif_body = 'Your card is now active'
    send_notification_C10()

def activation_failed_C10() -> None:
    """Handles failed card activation attempts."""
    ws_activation_attempts += 1
    if ws_activation_attempts >= 3:
        card_blocking_C10()
    ws_notif_type = 'activation_failed'
    send_notification_C10()

def pin_management_C10() -> None:
    """Handles PIN management procedures."""
    if ws_pin_change_request == 'Y':
        if validate_current_pin_C10():
            set_new_pin_C10()

def validate_current_pin_C10() -> bool:
    """Validates the current PIN."""
    pin_verify_result = pinverify(ws_card_number, ws_current_pin)
    if pin_verify_result == 'MATCH':
        return True
    else:
        ws_pin_attempts += 1
        if ws_pin_attempts >= 3:
            card_blocking_C10()
        return False

def set_new_pin_C10() -> None:
    """Sets a new PIN."""
    ws_encrypted_pin = pinenrypt(ws_new_pin)
    card_pin_block = ws_encrypted_pin
    card_pin_change_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_card_record(card_record)
    ws_notif_type = 'pin_changed'
    ws_notif_channel = 'SMS'
    ws_notif_body = 'Your PIN has been changed'
    send_notification_C10()

def card_replacement_C10() -> None:
    """Handles card replacement procedures."""
    if ws_replace_request == 'Y':
        cancel_old_card_C10()
        card_issuance_C10()
        ship_new_card_C10()

def cancel_old_card_C10() -> None:
    """Cancels the old card."""
    card_status = 'R'
    card_cancel_reason = 'REPLACED'
    card_cancel_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_card_record(card_record)

def ship_new_card_C10() -> None:
    """Ships the new card."""
    ws_shipment_record = ShipmentRecord()
    ws_shipment_record.ship_card_number = ws_card_number
    ws_shipment_record.ship_address = ws_cardholder_address
    if ws_expedite == 'Y':
        ws_shipment_record.ship_speed = 'EXPEDITED'
    else:
        ws_shipment_record.ship_speed = 'STANDARD'
    write_shipment_record(ws_shipment_record)

def card_blocking_C10() -> None:
    """Handles card blocking procedures."""
    card_status = 'B'
    card_block_reason = 'MULTIPLE FAILED ATTEMPTS'
    card_block_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_card_record(card_record)
    ws_notif_type = 'card_blocked'
    send_notification_C10()

def send_notification_C10() -> None:
    """Sends a notification."""
    pass

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """Card blocking procedure."""
    CARD_STATUS = 'B'
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_BLOCK_REASON
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    CARD_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_CARD_RECORD
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = 'Your card has been blocked: ' + WS_BLOCK_REASON
    send_notification_C11()

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """Wire transfer procedure."""
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """Validate wire request."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == "":
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """Screen against OFAC."""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, WS_WIRE_REJECT
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    ofacsrch_C11(OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE)
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    ofacsrch_C11(OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE)
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Process wire transfer."""
    debit_originator_C11()
    create_wire_message_C11()
    transmit_wire_C11()
    record_wire_C11()

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """Debit originator account."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def create_wire_message_C11() -> None:
    """Create SWIFT wire message."""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE
    WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE = SwiftMessageC11()
    SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103'
    SWIFT_TXN_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    SWIFT_VALUE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_DATE
    SWIFT_CURRENCY  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_CURRENCY
    SWIFT_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST  = None  # TODO: was WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME
    SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    SWIFT_BENEF_CUST  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    SWIFT_BENEF_BANK = WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC
    SWIFT_REMIT_INFO  = None  # TODO: was WS_PURPOSE

def transmit_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Transmit SWIFT wire message."""
    global WS_WIRE_STATUS
    swift_send_C11(WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE)
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11()

def record_wire_C11() -> None:

    #WRITE wire_record FROM ws_wire_record
    pass

def reverse_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Reverse the debit transaction."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def ach_processing_C11() -> None:
    """Process ACH transactions."""
    receive_ach_file_C11()
    validate_ach_entries_C11()
    process_ach_credits_C11()
    process_ach_debits_C11()
    generate_ach_return_C11()

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
    """Receive ACH input file."""
    #OPEN INPUT ach_input_file
    #READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_file_header
    pass
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_FILE_ID
    WS_ACH_FILE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_CREATION_DATE
    WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ENTRY_COUNT

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Validate ACH entries."""
    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
        pass
        if True: #AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else: #NOT AT END
            validate_single_entry_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def validate_single_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Validate a single ACH entry."""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not isinstance(ACH_ROUTING, int): #ACH_ROUTING NOT NUMERIC
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == "":
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        global WS_VALID_ENTRIES
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        global WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """Process ACH credit entries."""
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
        pass
        if True: #AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else: #NOT AT END
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ['22', '23', '32', '33']:
                apply_credit_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_credit_C11() -> None:
    """Apply an ACH credit to an account."""
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C11()
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def process_ach_debits_C11() -> None:
    """Process ACH debit entries."""
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
        pass
        if True: #AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else: #NOT AT END
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ['27', '28', '37', '38']:
                apply_debit_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Apply an ACH debit to an account."""
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ACH_AMOUNT:
            global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_DEBITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_DEBITS
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
            update_account_C11()
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'
            create_return_entry_C11()
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def generate_ach_return_C11() -> None:
    """Generate ACH return file."""
    if WS_RETURN_COUNT > 0:
        create_return_file_C11()

    global WS_RETURN_COUNT
    WS_RETURN_COUNT += 1
    #WRITE ach_return_record FROM ws_ach_return_entry
    pass

def statement_generation_C11() -> None:
    """Generate account statements."""
    prepare_statement_data_C11()
    generate_account_summary_C11()
    generate_transaction_detail_C11()
    calculate_statement_totals_C11()
    format_statement_C11()
    deliver_statement_C11()

def prepare_statement_data_C11() -> None:
    """Prepare data for statement generation."""
    WS_STMT_DATE = "current_date"  #FUNCTION current_date
    WS_STMT_START_DATE = int("integer_of_date(" + WS_STMT_DATE + ")") - 30 #FUNCTION integer_of_date(ws_stmt_date) - 30
    WS_STMT_END_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_DATE
    WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT = 0
    WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")

def generate_account_summary_C11() -> None:
    """Generate account summary section."""

    WS_STMT_SUMMARY = WsStmtSummaryC11()

    STMT_CLOSING_BAL  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE

def generate_transaction_detail_C11() -> None:
    """Generate transaction detail section."""
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #READ transaction_history INTO ws_trans_hist_rec
        pass
        if True: #AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else: #NOT AT END
            if HIST_ACCOUNT == ACCT_ID:
                if HIST_DATE >= WS_STMT_START_DATE:
                    add_transaction_line_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def add_transaction_line_C11() -> None:
    """Add a transaction line to the statement."""
    global WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT, WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL, WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT += 1
    #STMT_TRANS_DATE(WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT)  = None  # TODO: was HIST_DATE
    #STMT_TRANS_DESC(WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT)  = None  # TODO: was HIST_DESC
    #STMT_TRANS_AMT(WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT)  = None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT
    #STMT_TRANS_BAL(WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT)  = None  # TODO: was HIST_BALANCE
    pass
    if HIST_TYPE == 'C':
        WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT

def calculate_statement_totals_C11() -> None:
    """Calculate statement totals."""
    global STMT_TOTAL_CREDITS, STMT_TOTAL_DEBITS, STMT_NET_CHANGE
    STMT_TOTAL_CREDITS = WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL
    STMT_TOTAL_DEBITS  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    STMT_NET_CHANGE = WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL - WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    global WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT
    STMT_TRANS_COUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT
    if WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT > 0:
        global WS_TOTAL_DAILY_BALANCES
        STMT_AVG_DAILY_BAL = WS_TOTAL_DAILY_BALANCES / 30

def format_statement_C11() -> None:
    """Format the statement for delivery."""
    create_header_C11()
    create_summary_section_C11()
    create_transaction_list_C11()
    create_footer_C11()
