def deliver_statement_C11() -> None:
    """Deliver the generated statement."""
    if WS_DELIVERY_PREF == 'PAPER':
        print_statement_C11()
    elif WS_DELIVERY_PREF == 'EMAIL':
        email_statement_C11()
    elif WS_DELIVERY_PREF == 'BOTH':
        print_statement_C11()
        email_statement_C11()

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """Validate stop request."""
    global WS_STOP_VALID_C12, WS_STOP_REJECT_C12
    WS_STOP_VALID_C12 = 'Y'
    if WS_CHECK_NUMBER_C12 == 0:

    if WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED_C12 == 'Y':
        WS_STOP_VALID_C12 = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT_C12 = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

def box_rental_30100_C12() -> None:
    """Box rental."""
    if WS_RENTAL_REQUEST_C12 == 'Y':
        check_availability_30110_C12()
        if WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12 == 'Y':
            assign_box_30120_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_30130_C12()

def check_availability_30110_C12() -> None:
    """Check availability."""
    global WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX_C12
    WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12 = 'N'
    ws_box_idx: int
    for ws_box_idx in range(1, WS_TOTAL_BOXES_C12 + 1):
        if BOX_STATUS_C12[ws_box_idx - 1] == 'A':  # Assuming BOX_STATUS is a list
            if BOX_SIZE_C12[ws_box_idx - 1] == WS_REQUESTED_SIZE_C12:  # Assuming BOX_SIZE is a list
                WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12 = 'Y'
                WS_ASSIGNED_BOX_C12 = ws_box_idx
                break

    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C12 -= None  # TODO: was WS_FEE_AMOUNT_C12
    update_account_2350_C12()
    BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL_C12[ws_box_idx - 1] = BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL_C12[ws_box_idx - 1] + 10000  # Assuming BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL is a list

def process_authorization_31100_C12() -> None:
    """Process authorization."""
    validate_card_31110_C12()
    if WS_CARD_VALID_C12 == 'Y':
        check_fraud_score_31120_C12()
        if WS_FRAUD_APPROVED_C12 == 'Y':
            check_available_credit_31130_C12()
            if WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE_C12 == 'Y':
                approve_auth_31140_C12()
            else:
                decline_auth_31150_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_31150_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_31150_C12()

def validate_card_31110_C12() -> None:
    """Validate card."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID_C12
    WS_CARD_VALID_C12 = 'N'
    check_luhn_31115_C12()
    if WS_LUHN_VALID_C12 == 'Y':
        check_expiry_31116_C12()
        if WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12 == 'Y':
            check_cvv_31117_C12()
            if WS_CVV_VALID_C12 == 'Y':
                WS_CARD_VALID_C12 = 'Y'

def check_luhn_31115_C12() -> None:
    """Check luhn."""
    global WS_LUHN_VALID_C12, WS_LUHN_SUM_C12
    WS_LUHN_SUM_C12 = 0
    ws_luhn_idx: int
    for ws_luhn_idx in range(16, 0, -1):
        ws_luhn_digit: int = int(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER_C12[ws_luhn_idx - 1])  # Assuming WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER is a string
        if (17 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9
        WS_LUHN_SUM_C12 += ws_luhn_digit

    if WS_LUHN_SUM_C12 % 10 == 0:
        WS_LUHN_VALID_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_LUHN_VALID_C12 = 'N'

def check_expiry_31116_C12() -> None:
    """Check expiry."""
    global WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12
    if WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE_C12 >= WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12 = 'N'

def check_cvv_31117_C12() -> None:
    """Check cvv."""
    global WS_CVV_VALID_C12
    cvvverify_C12(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER_C12, WS_AUTH_CVV_C12, WS_CVV_RESULT_C12)
    if WS_CVV_RESULT_C12 == 'M':
        WS_CVV_VALID_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CVV_VALID_C12 = 'N'

    pass

def batch_transactions_31310_C12() -> None:
    """Batch transactions."""
    global WS_BATCH_TOTAL_C12, WS_BATCH_COUNT_C12, WS_EOF_FLAG_C12
    WS_BATCH_TOTAL_C12 = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_BATCH_COUNT_C12 = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG_C12 = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG_C12 == 'N':
        read_capture_file_C12()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG_C12 == 'Y':
            pass
        else:
            if CAPTURE_SETTLED_C12 == 'N':
                WS_BATCH_TOTAL_C12 += None  # TODO: was CAPTURE_AMOUNT_C12
                WS_BATCH_COUNT_C12 += 1
                CAPTURE_SETTLED_C12 = 'Y'
                rewrite_capture_record_31310_C12()
    WS_EOF_FLAG_C12 = 'N'

def calculate_fees_31320_C12() -> None:
    """Calculate fees."""
    global WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE_C12, WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE_C12, WS_PROCESSOR_FEE_C12, WS_TOTAL_FEES_C12
    WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE_C12 = WS_BATCH_TOTAL_C12 * Decimal('0.0175')
    WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE_C12 = WS_BATCH_TOTAL_C12 * Decimal('0.0015')
    WS_PROCESSOR_FEE_C12 = Decimal(WS_BATCH_COUNT_C12) * Decimal('0.10')
    WS_TOTAL_FEES_C12 = WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE_C12 + WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE_C12 + WS_PROCESSOR_FEE_C12

def create_funding_record_31330_C12() -> None:

    global WS_NET_FUNDING_C12, WS_FUNDING_RECORD_C12
    WS_NET_FUNDING_C12 = WS_BATCH_TOTAL_C12 - WS_TOTAL_FEES_C12
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD_C12 = WsFundingRecordC12()
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD_C12.funding_merchant  = None  # TODO: was WS_MERCHANT_ID_C12
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD_C12.funding_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_NET_FUNDING_C12
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD_C12.funding_fees  = None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES_C12
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD_C12.funding_date = int(WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12) + 2  # Assuming WS_PROCESS_DATE is an integer date
    write_funding_record_C12(WS_FUNDING_RECORD_C12)

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """Performs treasury management tasks."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the cash position."""
    global ws_cash_position
    ws_cash_position = Decimal("0.00")
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the vault cash."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_cash_position
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # vault_rec = read_vault_cash_file() # Assuming a function reads the file
            vault_balance = Decimal("100.00")  # example, replace with actual vault_rec data
            ws_cash_position += vault_balance
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the fed account balance."""
    global ws_cash_position, ws_fed_balance
    # ws_fed_balance = read_fed_account_file() # Assuming a function reads the file
    ws_fed_balance = Decimal("200.00") # Example value
    ws_cash_position += ws_fed_balance

def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """Sums correspondent bank balances."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_cash_position
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # corr_rec = read_correspondent_file() # Assuming a function reads the file
            corr_balance = Decimal("50.00")  # Example, replace with actual corr_rec data
            ws_cash_position += corr_balance
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects cash flows."""
    global ws_projected_inflows, ws_projected_outflows, ws_net_position, ws_cash_position
    ws_projected_inflows = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_projected_outflows = Decimal("0.00")
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    ws_net_position = ws_cash_position + ws_projected_inflows - ws_projected_outflows

def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """Projects loan payments."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_projected_inflows, ws_projection_date
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # loan_pmt_rec = read_loan_schedule_file() # Assuming a function reads the file
            loan_pmt_date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 1) #Example values
            loan_pmt_amount = Decimal("200.00")
            ws_projection_date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 2)
            if loan_pmt_date <= ws_projection_date:
                ws_projected_inflows += loan_pmt_amount
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects deposit flows."""
    global ws_expected_deposits, ws_expected_withdrawals, ws_projected_inflows, ws_projected_outflows, ws_avg_daily_deposits, ws_avg_daily_withdrawals, ws_projection_days
    ws_avg_daily_deposits = Decimal("1000.00")
    ws_projection_days = 30
    ws_avg_daily_withdrawals = Decimal("800.00")
    ws_expected_deposits = ws_avg_daily_deposits * ws_projection_days
    ws_expected_withdrawals = ws_avg_daily_withdrawals * ws_projection_days
    ws_projected_inflows += ws_expected_deposits
    ws_projected_outflows += ws_expected_withdrawals

def project_investment_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Projects investment maturities."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_projected_inflows, ws_projection_date
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # inv_rec = read_investment_file() # Assuming a function reads the file
            inv_maturity_date = datetime.date(2024, 2, 1) #Example values
            inv_par_value = Decimal("10000.00")
            ws_projection_date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 2)
            if inv_maturity_date <= ws_projection_date:
                ws_projected_inflows += inv_par_value
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def manage_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Manages reserves."""
    calculate_reserve_requirement_C13()
    check_reserve_position_C13()
    if ws_reserve_deficiency == 'Y':
        cover_reserve_shortfall_C13()
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves_C13()

def calculate_reserve_requirement_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the reserve requirement."""
    global ws_reserve_requirement, ws_total_deposits, ws_reserve_ratio
    ws_total_deposits = Decimal("1000000.00")
    ws_reserve_ratio = Decimal("0.10")
    ws_reserve_requirement = ws_total_deposits * ws_reserve_ratio

def check_reserve_position_C13() -> None:
    """Checks the reserve position."""
    global ws_excess_reserves, ws_fed_balance, ws_reserve_requirement, ws_reserve_deficiency
    ws_fed_balance = Decimal("120000.00")
    ws_excess_reserves = ws_fed_balance - ws_reserve_requirement
    if ws_excess_reserves < 0:
        ws_reserve_deficiency = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_reserve_deficiency = 'N'

def cover_reserve_shortfall_C13() -> None:
    """Covers a reserve shortfall."""
    global ws_shortfall_amount, ws_excess_reserves
    ws_shortfall_amount = 0 - ws_excess_reserves
    borrow_fed_funds_C13()

def borrow_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Borrows fed funds."""
    # initialize_ws_fed_funds_transaction() #Assuming this initializes a data object
    global ff_trans_type, ws_shortfall_amount, ff_amount, ws_fed_funds_rate, ff_rate, ws_process_date, ff_settle_date, ff_maturity_date
    ff_trans_type = 'BORROW'
    ff_amount = ws_shortfall_amount
    ws_fed_funds_rate = Decimal("0.05")
    ff_rate = ws_fed_funds_rate
    ws_process_date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 2)
    ff_settle_date = ws_process_date
    ff_maturity_date = (ws_process_date + datetime.timedelta(days=1))
    # write_fed_funds_record(ws_fed_funds_transaction) # Assuming a function to write the record
    pass

def invest_excess_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Invests excess reserves."""
    global ws_excess_reserves, ws_min_invest_amount
    ws_min_invest_amount = Decimal("10000.00")
    if ws_excess_reserves > ws_min_invest_amount:
        sell_fed_funds_C13()

def sell_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Sells fed funds."""
    # initialize_ws_fed_funds_transaction()
    global ff_trans_type, ws_excess_reserves, ff_amount, ws_fed_funds_rate, ff_rate, ws_process_date, ff_settle_date, ff_maturity_date
    ff_trans_type = 'SELL'
    ff_amount = ws_excess_reserves
    ws_fed_funds_rate = Decimal("0.05")
    ff_rate = ws_fed_funds_rate
    ws_process_date = datetime.date(2024, 1, 2)
    ff_settle_date = ws_process_date
    ff_maturity_date = (ws_process_date + datetime.timedelta(days=1))
    # write_fed_funds_record(ws_fed_funds_transaction)
    pass

def manage_investments_C13() -> None:
    """Manages investments."""
    review_investment_portfolio_C13()
    execute_investment_strategy_C13()
    mark_to_market_C13()

def review_investment_portfolio_C13() -> None:
    """Reviews the investment portfolio."""
    global ws_investment_pool, ws_avg_yield, ws_avg_duration, ws_eof_flag, ws_total_yield, ws_total_duration, ws_inv_count
    ws_investment_pool = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_avg_yield = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_avg_duration = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_yield = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_duration = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_inv_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # inv_rec = read_investment_file() # Assuming a function reads the file
            inv_market_value = Decimal("10000.00") #Example values
            inv_yield = Decimal("0.05")
            inv_duration = Decimal("3.00")

            ws_investment_pool += inv_market_value
            ws_total_yield += inv_yield
            ws_total_duration += inv_duration
            ws_inv_count += 1
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_inv_count > 0:
        ws_avg_yield = ws_total_yield / ws_inv_count
        ws_avg_duration = ws_total_duration / ws_inv_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def execute_investment_strategy_C13() -> None:
    """Executes the investment strategy."""
    if ws_rate_outlook == 'RISING':
        shorten_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'FALLING':
        extend_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'STABLE':
        maintain_position_C13()

def shorten_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Shortens the portfolio duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: SHORTENING PORTFOLIO DURATION')

def extend_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Extends the portfolio duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: EXTENDING PORTFOLIO DURATION')

def maintain_position_C13() -> None:
    """Maintains the current portfolio position."""
    print('STRATEGY: MAINTAINING CURRENT POSITION')

def mark_to_market_C13() -> None:
    """Marks investments to market value."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # inv_rec = read_investment_file() # Assuming a function reads the file
            inv_cusip = "123456789" #Example values
            inv_par_value = Decimal("10000.00")
            inv_book_value = Decimal("9900.00")

            get_market_price_C13(inv_cusip)
            inv_market_value = inv_par_value * ws_market_price / 100
            inv_unrealized_gl = inv_market_value - inv_book_value
            # rewrite_investment_record(inv_rec) #Assuming a function rewrites the record in the investment file
            pass

        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    """Gets the market price for a bond."""
    global ws_cusip_lookup, ws_market_price
    ws_cusip_lookup = inv_cusip
    # call_bondprice(ws_cusip_lookup, ws_market_price) #Assuming there is a BondPrice class or function

def review_borrowing_capacity_C13() -> None:
    """Reviews the borrowing capacity."""
    global ws_borrowing_capacity, ws_fhlb_capacity, ws_repo_capacity, ws_credit_line_avail
    ws_borrowing_capacity = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_fhlb_capacity = Decimal("5000000.00")
    ws_repo_capacity = Decimal("3000000.00")
    ws_credit_line_avail = Decimal("2000000.00")
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_fhlb_capacity
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_repo_capacity
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_credit_line_avail

def optimize_funding_mix_C13() -> None:
    """Optimizes the funding mix."""
    global ws_deposit_cost, ws_total_int_expense, ws_total_deposits, ws_wholesale_rate
    ws_total_int_expense = Decimal("50000.00")
    ws_total_deposits = Decimal("1000000.00")
    ws_wholesale_rate = Decimal("0.04")

    ws_deposit_cost = (ws_total_int_expense / ws_total_deposits) * 100
    if ws_deposit_cost > ws_wholesale_rate:
        print('CONSIDER WHOLESALE FUNDING')

def manage_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Manages borrowing maturities."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_process_date
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    ws_process_date = datetime.date(2024,1,2)
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # borrow_rec = read_borrowing_file() # Assuming a function reads the file
            borrow_maturity = datetime.date(2024, 1, 8)
            borrow_amount = Decimal("1000000.00")
            if borrow_maturity <= (ws_process_date + datetime.timedelta(days=7)):
                rollover_decision_C13(borrow_maturity, borrow_amount)
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def rollover_decision_C13(borrow_maturity:datetime.date, borrow_amount:Decimal) -> None:
    """Determines whether to rollover or repay a borrowing."""
    global ws_cash_position
    ws_cash_position = Decimal("1200000.00")
    if ws_cash_position >= borrow_amount:
        repay_borrowing_C13(borrow_amount)
    else:
        rollover_borrowing_C13()

def repay_borrowing_C13(borrow_amount:Decimal) -> None:
    """Repays a borrowing."""
    global ws_cash_position
    ws_cash_position -= borrow_amount
    borrow_status = 'REPAID'
    # rewrite_borrowing_record(borrow_rec) #Assuming there is a function to rewrite the borrowing record
    pass

def rollover_borrowing_C13() -> None:
    """Rolls over a borrowing."""
    global ws_process_date, ws_current_rate
    borrow_rollover_date = ws_process_date
    borrow_maturity = (ws_process_date + datetime.timedelta(days=30))
    ws_current_rate = Decimal("0.06")
    borrow_rate = ws_current_rate
    # rewrite_borrowing_record(borrow_rec) #Assuming there is a function to rewrite the borrowing record
    pass

    calculate_nsfr_C13()
    calculate_basic_ratio_C13()

def calculate_lcr_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the Liquidity Coverage Ratio (LCR)."""
    global ws_lcr_ratio, ws_lcr_numerator, ws_lcr_denominator
    sum_hqla_C13()
    calculate_net_outflows_C13()
    if ws_lcr_denominator > 0:
        ws_lcr_ratio = (ws_lcr_numerator / ws_lcr_denominator) * 100

def sum_hqla_C13() -> None:
    """Sums High-Quality Liquid Assets (HQLA) for LCR calculation."""
    global ws_lcr_numerator, ws_eof_flag
    ws_lcr_numerator = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            # inv_rec = read_investment_file() # Assuming a function reads the file

            inv_hqla_level = "1" #Example values
            inv_market_value = Decimal("10000.00")

            if inv_hqla_level == '1':
                ws_lcr_numerator += inv_market_value
            elif inv_hqla_level == '2A':
                ws_adjusted_value = inv_market_value * Decimal("0.85")
                ws_lcr_numerator += ws_adjusted_value
            elif inv_hqla_level == '2B':
                ws_adjusted_value = inv_market_value * Decimal("0.50")
                ws_lcr_numerator += ws_adjusted_value

        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_net_outflows_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates net cash outflows for LCR calculation."""
    global ws_total_outflows, ws_total_inflows, ws_lcr_denominator, ws_stable_deposits, ws_less_stable_deposits, ws_operational_deposits, ws_non_operational

import datetime

def capital_management_C14() -> None:
    """Performs capital management procedures."""
    calculate_capital_ratios_C14()
    risk_weighted_assets_C14()
    capital_planning_C14()
    stress_testing_C14()

def calculate_capital_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates capital ratios."""
    calculate_tier1_C14()
    calculate_tier2_C14()
    calculate_ratios_C14()

def calculate_tier1_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates Tier 1 capital."""
    global ws_tier1_capital
    ws_tier1_capital = 0
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_common_stock
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_retained_earnings
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_aoci
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_goodwill
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_intangibles
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_dta_deduction

def calculate_tier2_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates Tier 2 capital."""
    global ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital
    ws_tier2_capital = 0
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_sub_debt
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_alll_eligible
    ws_total_capital = ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital

def calculate_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates capital ratios."""
    global ws_cet1_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_leverage_ratio
    if ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0:
        ws_cet1_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
        ws_capital_ratio = (ws_total_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_leverage_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_total_assets) * 100

def risk_weighted_assets_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates risk-weighted assets."""
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = 0
    credit_rwa_C14()
    market_rwa_C14()
    operational_rwa_C14()

def credit_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates credit risk-weighted assets."""
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_cash_rwa, ws_govt_rwa, ws_bank_rwa, ws_mortgage_rwa, ws_commercial_rwa, ws_consumer_rwa
    ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * 0.00
    ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * 0.00
    ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * 0.20
    ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * 0.50
    ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * 1.00
    ws_consumer_rwa = ws_consumer_loans * 1.00
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_cash_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_govt_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_bank_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_mortgage_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_commercial_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_consumer_rwa

def market_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates market risk-weighted assets."""
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_market_rwa
    ws_market_rwa = ws_trading_assets * ws_market_risk_factor
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_market_rwa

def operational_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates operational risk-weighted assets."""
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_operational_rwa
    ws_operational_rwa = ws_gross_income * ws_operational_factor * 12.5
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_operational_rwa

def capital_planning_C14() -> None:
    """Performs capital planning."""
    project_capital_needs_C14()
    identify_capital_actions_C14()
    update_capital_plan_C14()

def project_capital_needs_C14() -> None:
    """Projects capital needs."""
    global ws_projected_rwa, ws_required_capital, ws_capital_gap
    ws_projected_rwa = ws_risk_weighted_assets * (1 + ws_growth_rate)
    ws_required_capital = ws_projected_rwa * ws_target_ratio / 100
    ws_capital_gap = ws_required_capital - ws_total_capital

def identify_capital_actions_C14() -> None:
    """Identifies capital actions."""
    global ws_capital_action
    if ws_capital_gap > 0:
        if ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj:
            ws_capital_action = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity:
            ws_capital_action = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            ws_capital_action = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        ws_capital_action = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

def update_capital_plan_C14() -> None:
    """Updates the capital plan."""
    ws_plan_update_date = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    plan_recommended_action = ws_capital_action
    plan_gap_amount = ws_capital_gap
    # Assume rewrite_capital_plan_record(ws_capital_plan) is handled elsewhere
    pass

def stress_testing_C14() -> None:
    """Performs stress testing."""
    run_baseline_C14()
    run_adverse_C14()
    run_severely_adverse_C14()
    compile_results_C14()

def run_baseline_C14() -> None:
    """Runs the baseline scenario."""
    global ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline
    ws_scenario_name = 'BASELINE'
    ws_rate_shock = 0.00
    ws_gdp_change = 2.50
    ws_unemployment_rate = 4.00
    ws_housing_decline = 0.00
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def run_adverse_C14() -> None:
    """Runs the adverse scenario."""
    global ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline
    ws_scenario_name = 'ADVERSE'
    ws_rate_shock = 2.00
    ws_gdp_change = -1.50
    ws_unemployment_rate = 7.00
    ws_housing_decline = -15.00
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def run_severely_adverse_C14() -> None:
    """Runs the severely adverse scenario."""
    global ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline
    ws_scenario_name = 'severely_adverse'
    ws_rate_shock = 3.00
    ws_gdp_change = -6.00
    ws_unemployment_rate = 10.00
    ws_housing_decline = -30.00
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def compile_results_C14() -> None:
    """Compiles stress test results."""
    print('STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED')
    if ws_stress_pass_fail == 'FAIL':
        remediation_actions_C14()

def calculate_stress_impact_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates the impact of the stress scenario."""
    global ws_credit_losses, ws_market_losses, ws_stress_losses, ws_stressed_capital, ws_stressed_ratio, ws_stress_pass_fail
    ws_credit_losses = ws_loan_portfolio * ws_stress_lgd * ws_stress_pd
    ws_market_losses = ws_trading_assets * ws_rate_shock / 100
    ws_stress_losses = ws_credit_losses + ws_market_losses
    ws_stressed_capital = ws_total_capital - ws_stress_losses
    ws_stressed_ratio = (ws_stressed_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    if ws_stressed_ratio >= ws_min_capital_ratio:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'PASS'
    else:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'FAIL'

def remediation_actions_C14() -> None:
    """Takes remediation actions after a stress test failure."""
    ws_notif_type = 'stress_failure'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'URGENT: Stress test failure - action required'
    send_notification_C14() # Placeholder for external function

def general_ledger_C14() -> None:
    """Performs general ledger procedures."""
    post_journal_entry_C14()
    balance_gl_C14()
    close_period_C14()
    generate_trial_balance_C14()

def post_journal_entry_C14() -> None:
    """Posts a journal entry."""
    validate_journal_entry_C14()
    if ws_je_valid == 'Y':
        post_to_accounts_C14()
        record_posting_C14()

def validate_journal_entry_C14() -> None:
    """Validates a journal entry."""
    global ws_je_valid, ws_total_debits, ws_total_credits, ws_je_error
    ws_je_valid = 'Y'
    ws_total_debits = 0
    ws_total_credits = 0
    for ws_je_idx in range(1, 51):
        ws_total_debits += je_debit[ws_je_idx - 1]
        ws_total_credits += je_credit[ws_je_idx - 1]
    if ws_total_debits != ws_total_credits:
        ws_je_valid = 'N'
        ws_je_error = 'OUT OF BALANCE'

def post_to_accounts_C14() -> None:
    """Posts the journal entry to the GL accounts."""
    for ws_je_idx in range(1, 51):
        if je_gl_account[ws_je_idx - 1] != '':
            ws_gl_account = je_gl_account[ws_je_idx - 1]
            # Assuming read_gl_master_file and ws_gl_record update
            ws_gl_debit_balance += je_debit[ws_je_idx - 1]
            ws_gl_credit_balance += je_credit[ws_je_idx - 1]
            ws_gl_net_balance = ws_gl_debit_balance - ws_gl_credit_balance
            # Assuming rewrite_gl_record(ws_gl_record) handles writing back to the GL
            pass

def record_posting_C14() -> None:
    """Records the journal entry posting."""
    global ws_je_status, ws_je_post_date
    ws_je_status = 'POSTED'
    ws_je_post_date = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    # Assuming write_journal_record(ws_journal_entry) handles writing to the journal
    pass

def balance_gl_C14() -> None:
    """Balances the general ledger."""
    global ws_total_assets, ws_total_liabilities, ws_total_equity, ws_eof_flag, ws_balance_check, ws_error_msg
    ws_total_assets = 0
    ws_total_liabilities = 0
    ws_total_equity = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assume read_gl_master_file into WS_GL_RECORD and update WS_EOF_FLAG
        #Simulate end of file after some iterations
        #ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        #
        #Replace with actual read if file structure is known
        try:
           #read gl record here
           pass
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

        if ws_eof_flag == 'N':
            if gl_asset:
                ws_total_assets += ws_gl_net_balance
            elif gl_liability:
                ws_total_liabilities += ws_gl_net_balance
            elif gl_equity:
                ws_total_equity += ws_gl_net_balance

    ws_balance_check = ws_total_assets - ws_total_liabilities - ws_total_equity
    if ws_balance_check != 0:
        ws_error_msg = 'GL OUT OF BALANCE'
        handle_error_C14() # Placeholder for external error handling

def close_period_C14() -> None:
    """Closes the accounting period."""
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        close_revenue_expense_C14()
        update_retained_earnings_C14()
        record_close_C14()

def close_revenue_expense_C14() -> None:
    """Closes revenue and expense accounts to retained earnings."""
    global ws_net_income, ws_eof_flag
    ws_net_income = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assume read_gl_master_file into WS_GL_RECORD and update WS_EOF_FLAG
        #Simulate end of file after some iterations
        #ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        #
        #Replace with actual read if file structure is known
        try:
           #read gl record here
           pass
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

        if ws_eof_flag == 'N':
            if gl_revenue:
                ws_net_income += ws_gl_net_balance
                ws_gl_debit_balance = 0
                ws_gl_credit_balance = 0
                ws_gl_net_balance = 0
                # Assume rewrite_gl_record(WS_GL_RECORD) handles writing the updated record
                pass
            if gl_expense:
                ws_net_income -= ws_gl_net_balance
                ws_gl_debit_balance = 0
                ws_gl_credit_balance = 0
                ws_gl_net_balance = 0
                # Assume rewrite_gl_record(WS_GL_RECORD) handles writing the updated record
                pass

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def update_retained_earnings_C14() -> None:
    """Updates retained earnings with the net income."""
    global ws_gl_credit_balance, ws_gl_net_balance
    ws_gl_account = ws_retained_earnings_acct
    # Assuming read_gl_master_file into WS_GL_RECORD and update WS_GL_CREDIT_BALANCE
    ws_gl_credit_balance += ws_net_income
    ws_gl_net_balance = ws_gl_credit_balance - ws_gl_debit_balance
    # Assume rewrite_gl_record(WS_GL_RECORD) handles writing the updated record
    pass

def record_close_C14() -> None:
    """Records the period closing information."""
    global close_date, close_net_income, close_status
    # Assuming WS_PERIOD_CLOSE_REC is initialized elsewhere correctly
    close_date = ws_process_date
    close_net_income = ws_net_income
    close_status = 'CLOSED'
    # Assuming write_period_close_record(WS_PERIOD_CLOSE_REC) handles writing the record
    pass

def validate_call_report_C14() -> None:
    """Validates the Call Report data."""
    run_validity_checks_C14()
    run_quality_checks_C14()

def run_validity_checks_C14() -> None:
    """Runs validity checks on the Call Report."""
    global ws_validity_errors
    ws_validity_errors = 0
    # Assume RC_OTHER_ASSETS is defined
    if rc_total_assets != rc_total_loans + rc_securities + rc_other_assets:
        ws_validity_errors += 1

def run_quality_checks_C14() -> None:
    """Runs quality checks on the Call Report."""
    global ws_quality_errors
    ws_quality_errors = 0
    if rc_total_assets < ws_prior_total_assets * 0.80:
        ws_quality_errors += 1

def submit_call_report_C14() -> None:
    """Submits the Call Report."""
    global ws_report_status
    if ws_validity_errors == 0:
        ws_report_status = 'SUBMITTED'
    else:
        ws_report_status = 'ERRORS'

def perform_37310_load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_ic_count_C15, ws_eof_flag_C15, intercompany_file_C15, ws_ic_balance_C15, ws_ic_array_C15
    ws_ic_count_C15 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_ic_balance_C15 = "READ " + intercompany_file_C15
            ws_ic_count_C15 += 1
            ws_ic_array_C15.append(ws_ic_balance_C15)
        except:
            ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

def perform_37320_match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_ic_idx_C15, ws_ic_count_C15
    ws_ic_idx_C15 = 1
    while ws_ic_idx_C15 <= ws_ic_count_C15:
        perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart_C15()
        ws_ic_idx_C15 += 1

def perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart_C15() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ic_from_entity_C15, ws_ic_idx_C15, ws_search_from_C15, ic_to_entity_C15, ws_search_to_C15, ws_ic_idx2_C15, ws_ic_count_C15, ic_amount_C15, ws_ic_diff_C15
    ws_search_from_C15 = ic_from_entity_C15[ws_ic_idx_C15 - 1]
    ws_search_to_C15 = ic_to_entity_C15[ws_ic_idx_C15 - 1]
    ws_ic_idx2_C15 = 1
    while ws_ic_idx2_C15 <= ws_ic_count_C15:
        if ic_from_entity_C15[ws_ic_idx2_C15 - 1] == ws_search_to_C15:
            if ic_to_entity_C15[ws_ic_idx2_C15 - 1] == ws_search_from_C15:
                ws_ic_diff_C15 = ic_amount_C15[ws_ic_idx_C15 - 1] + ic_amount_C15[ws_ic_idx2_C15 - 1]
                if ws_ic_diff_C15 != Decimal(0):
                    perform_37326_log_ic_diff_C15()
                break
        ws_ic_idx2_C15 += 1

def perform_37326_log_ic_diff_C15() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_ic_diff_rec_C15, ws_search_from_C15, ws_search_to_C15, ws_ic_diff_C15, ic_diff_record_C15
    ws_ic_diff_rec_C15 = WsIcDiffRecC15()
    ws_ic_diff_rec_C15.icd_from = ws_search_from_C15
    ws_ic_diff_rec_C15.icd_to = ws_search_to_C15
    ws_ic_diff_rec_C15.icd_amount = ws_ic_diff_C15
    ic_diff_record_C15 = str(ws_ic_diff_rec_C15)

def perform_38410_move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C15, audit_file_C15, ws_audit_record_C15, ws_audit_timestamp_C15, ws_archive_date_C15, archive_audit_record_C15
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_audit_record_C15 = "READ " + audit_file_C15
            if ws_audit_timestamp_C15 < ws_archive_date_C15:
                archive_audit_record_C15 = str(ws_audit_record_C15)
                pass # Placeholder for DELETE audit_file, not directly translatable
        except:
            ws_eof_flag_from decimal import Decimal

C15 = 'Y'
ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

    if ws_cpu_utilization_C15 > 80:

def perform_39140_transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_tps_C15, ws_trans_count_C15, ws_elapsed_seconds_C15, ws_avg_response_C15, ws_total_response_time_C15
    if ws_elapsed_seconds_C15 != 0:
        ws_tps_C15 = Decimal(ws_trans_count_C15 / ws_elapsed_seconds_C15)
    else:
        ws_tps_C15 = Decimal(0)
    if ws_trans_count_C15 != 0:
        ws_avg_response_C15 = Decimal(ws_total_response_time_C15 / ws_trans_count_C15)
    else:
        ws_avg_response_C15 = Decimal(0)

def perform_39200_analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_avg_response_C15, ws_response_threshold_C15, ws_perf_degraded_C15, ws_tps_C15, ws_min_tps_threshold_C15, ws_throughput_low_C15
    if ws_avg_response_C15 > ws_response_threshold_C15:
        ws_perf_degraded_C15 = 'Y'
    if ws_tps_C15 < ws_min_tps_threshold_C15:
        ws_throughput_low_C15 = 'Y'
